Trust

Axioms come from the Lean kernel (Lean (version 4.33.1, x86_64-w64-windows-gnu, commit 819816b2e0a3bf405af45ae5c7af2491d8f5bee6, Release)): the constants each declaration depends on, as Lean's collectAxioms reports them. Standard axioms are propext, Classical.choice and Quot.sound.

Lake environment and dependency-pin digest: 19ed0637b602a68c.

Verification scope: all declaration-bearing files; 1696 of 1696 eligible files selected, 9619 declarations requested.

Rendering mode: clean content-bound cache results only; 353 selected files had no reusable result and remain UNKNOWN. No Lean process was started for those misses during this static-site build.

Per-file probe bounds: 300 seconds. A resource stop is UNKNOWN, never a failed proof.

What the badges mean

no axioms
Lean's kernel checked the proof, and it rests on nothing but Lean's rules.
standard axioms
Checked by the kernel, using only propext, Classical.choice or Quot.sound: the three axioms nearly all of mathematics in Lean uses.
extra axioms
Checked by the kernel, but it assumes something the project declared with `axiom`. It is only as true as those assumptions.
depends on sorry
Not proved: somewhere in it, or in something it uses, a proof was left out (`sorry`).
trust unknown
Lean did not check it: Lean was not run, the file did not elaborate, or Lean has no constant by that name.
